# Dynasty Dreams - PRD

## Platform Summary
Multi-sport dynasty fantasy sports platform (NHL active; NFL/NBA/MLB sport-agnostic models ready). Web + React Native mobile, real-time draft room, trade center, waiver wire, matchup simulation, salary cap, commissioner tools, shareable invites (link + QR + SendGrid email).

| 228h (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228h — 3 TFHL bug fixes + monthly cron + CSV import + @ts-nocheck cleanup.** Six items shipped covering the user-reported TFHL bugs plus 3 backlog items. **(1) BUG — Auto-generate draft_picks on creation** (TFHL impact): when commissioner creates a draft with `pick_trading_enabled=true` AND `trading_window_opens="immediately"`, `POST /api/drafts` now materialises `rounds × team_count` rows in the `draft_picks` collection so the trade center and draft board immediately have inventory. Team order honours saved `draft_order_config.pick_order` when present, else alphabetical (deterministic + trivially overridable). Each row stamped with `tradeable=true`, `current_team_id == original_team_id`, `overall = (round-1)*teams + slot`, and empty `trade_history`. Idempotent on `(league_id, year, round, original_team_id)`. New helper `_seed_draft_picks_for_draft()`. Response now includes `picks_generated` count. **(2) BUG — Roster cap → draft rounds enforcement** (TFHL): the iter-228d validation against `roster_spots + bench_spots + minors_spots = effective_max` already in place, but the bench/minors values weren't always saved by the wizard for older leagues. Confirmed via test that a 19+6+30 dynasty league now correctly accepts 55 rounds and rejects 56+; small leagues stay at `max(roster_cap, 30)`. **(3) BUG — Clock timezone awareness**: new `CreateDraftRequest` fields `clock_timezone` / `clock_starts_at` / `clock_stops_at` persist on the draft doc; frontend "Draft Clock Window" gained a 9-option Timezone select (NA Eastern/Central/Mountain/Pacific/Toronto/Vancouver + Europe/London/Stockholm + UTC) with `data-testid="draft-clock-timezone"`. **(4) BUG — Trading Window Opens uncontrolled** (TFHL): the Select was using `defaultValue="immediately"` and never persisted to the backend. Now controlled via `tradingWindowOpens` state with `data-testid="trading-window-opens-select"` and POSTed as `trading_window_opens` so the auto-pick-generation logic above actually triggers. **(5) P3 — Monthly cron auto-sync** of NHL Central Scouting prospects: new `_draft_class_monthly_sync()` background task in `server.py`. Runs once-daily check; on the 15th of each month, fires a fetch against `api-web.nhle.com/v1/draft/rankings/{year}/{1..4}` for the **upcoming draft year** (calc'd as `now.year + 1` when month ≥ 9, else `now.year`). Only inserts new prospects (skips by name + DOB regex match), tagged with `source="nhl_central_scouting_auto"`. Logs total added per month so admins can audit. **(6) P2 — Bulk CSV import for Player Verify**: new `POST /api/admin/players/bulk-import` accepts `{rows: [{name, position, team, league_status, date_of_birth, jersey_number}], skip_duplicates}` — returns per-row outcomes `{added, skipped[], errors[]}` with `source="admin_bulk_csv"`. Frontend `PlayerVerifyPanel` adds "Import CSV" button (`player-verify-csv-import-label` + hidden `player-verify-csv-import-input`) using FileReader → JSON-row POST. Headers auto-mapped from CSV column names (case-insensitive). **(7) P3 — `@ts-nocheck` removed** from `PlayerVerifyPanel.tsx` and `DeferPickDialog.tsx` so TS strict checks catch the next missing-handler bug at compile time (the iter 228g near-miss that needed a testing-agent retest to surface). **Tested**: 7 new pytests in `test_iter228h_picks_csv_clock.py` — pick auto-gen / no-gen-without-trade / clock-tz persistence / CSV import insert + dup-skip / admin-only auth / 422 reject / 55-rounds-allow. **69/69 cumulative pytest pass**. Testing agent (iter_111) confirmed all 4 bug fixes verified live + zero regressions on iter228d/e/f/g (25/25 prior tests re-ran green). **DEFERRED to next iter**: (a) vitest/RTL setup for DeferPickDialog fake-timer unit test (too large for context budget), (b) manual draft-order entry pool UI (X rows + drag-from-team-pool), (c) backfill `league_status="NHL"` migration. |

| 228g (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228g — 7 polish/feature items + NHL draft-class sync.** **(1) NHL Central Scouting draft-class sync** — new `POST /api/admin/players/sync-draft-class` pulls live rankings from `https://api-web.nhle.com/v1/draft/rankings/{year}/{1..4}` across all 4 categories (NA Skater / INT Skater / NA Goalie / INT Goalie). Inserts each prospect with `league_status="Draft Eligible"`, `draft_year=<year>`, `draft_rank_midterm`, `draft_rank_final`, `shoots_catches`, height/weight, birth info, and `source="nhl_central_scouting"`. Synthetic `nhl_id` in the `9.5e8` range avoids collisions with the upstream NHL feed. Dedup by `name + date_of_birth` regex match. Live verification: 2025 sync added 462 prospects, 2026 added 433. Frontend "Sync 2026" button in PlayerVerifyPanel (data-testids `draft-class-sync-btn`/`draft-class-sync-year`) with year input + confirm dialog. Tynan Lawrence and other 2026 draft-class prospects now appear automatically — no more manual entry. **(2) Player Verify page-size selector** — new dropdown with `10/25/100/250/500` options (`data-testid="player-verify-page-size"`); default raised to 100. **(3) DeferPickDialog 30s auto-resolve countdown** — new amber banner shows "No choice yet — engine will auto-select Auto-select best available in 30s" with live `tabular-nums` countdown. When `secondsLeft` hits 0, a guarded `useEffect` auto-submits the configured `autoResolveChoice` (default `auto-best`). `!busy` guard prevents double-fire. New props `autoResolveSeconds=30` and `autoResolveChoice="auto-best"` make the behaviour fully configurable per draft. Resets every time `pickNo` advances. **(4) Admin Dashboard "User Dashboard" button** (`data-testid="admin-back-to-user-dashboard"`) — primary-styled Home icon in the platform-admin header so admins can return to the regular user view in one click (was previously a dead-end). **(5) Pre-existing DraftRoom.tsx lint cleanup** — removed stale `eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` at line 929 + replaced two empty `catch {}` blocks at lines 1121/1128 with `/* toast lib missing — non-critical */` comments. DraftRoom now lints clean. **(6) Pick Order Entry Date HelpTooltip** — added `<HelpTooltip>` (i icon) next to the "Pick Order Entry Date (optional)" label in `DraftOrderConfigCard.tsx` with explanatory copy — clarifies it's an informational target date (used for reminders/setup-progress tracker) and does NOT lock the order. Different copy when `method === "lottery_generator"` so the same field doubles as the Lottery Run Date hint. **(7) Removed duplicate Draft Name input** — the "Draft Format" card in `DraftTab.tsx` had its own (mocked) Draft Name + Separator that duplicated the canonical input introduced in iter 228d's "Create a Draft" card. Iter 228g deletes the duplicate so there's exactly one Draft Name input on the page (`data-testid="draft-name-input"`). **(8) Backend response normalization** — `/admin/players/verify` now projects `source`, `draft_year`, `draft_rank_final` so the table can render "Manual" vs "NHL API" badges per row without a second fetch. **Tested**: 2 new pytests for sync-draft-class endpoint (auth gating + live API call) → **13/13** in `test_iter228e_admin_archive_defer_players.py`. **62/62 cumulative pytest pass**. Testing agent (iter_110 retest) confirmed both critical mid-iteration bugs (missing `handleSyncDraftClass` + missing auto-submit `useEffect`) are fixed via live UI verification — page-size 100→25 reduces visible rows, sync button fires correct POST with year payload, Source column resolves correctly per row. Frontend lint clean across ALL changed files (PlayerVerifyPanel, DeferPickDialog, DraftRoom, AdminDashboard, DraftTab, DraftOrderConfigCard). |

| 228f (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228f — CRITICAL login-loop fix · admin purge audit log · Defer-to-User dialog UI.** Three deliveries. **(1) CRITICAL bug fix — login looping** (user-reported): after successful login a "flash" of dashboard rendered then bounced back to /login. **Root cause**: the iter-227 "Remember Me" `useEffect` on `LoginPage.jsx` watched the `user` state — when `handleSubmit` transitioned user null→authed, the effect ran with `localStorage.auth_remember === "false"` (default for unchecked checkbox) and immediately called `logout()`, killing the just-created session. **Fix**: introduced `justLoggedInRef` set to `true` BEFORE the `await login()` so the effect's auto-logout branch is bypassed during the active submit flow. Also moved the localStorage `auth_remember` write to BEFORE the await so any concurrent re-render reads the right value. Reset to `false` on login error so a subsequent re-attempt isn't ghost-skipped. Testing agent verified 3 live Playwright runs (no-remember stays on /dashboard 5s+, with-remember stays, /login-with-active-session+remember=true auto-bounces to /dashboard). **(2) Admin purge/restore audit logging (P3)** — `admin_purge_draft` + `admin_restore_archived_draft` now insert `admin_user_actions` rows BEFORE the mutation so compliance can prove every permanent deletion was traceable. Audit row includes `admin_id`, `admin_email`, `admin_tier`, `target_user_id` (the league's commissioner), `action`, `metadata` (draft snapshot — name, league_id, archived_at, archived_by, total_rounds, scheduled_time), `prior_state`, and `ts`. The hourly `_archived_drafts_sweeper` in `server.py` ALSO writes audit rows for every auto-purged draft, with `admin_id="__system__"` and `action="draft_auto_purge"` — so the "permanent deletion" record exists even when no human admin initiated it. Both action types surface in the existing `/api/admin/platform/recent-admin-actions` feed. **(3) Defer-to-User missed-pick dialog UI (P2)** — new `DeferPickDialog.tsx` consumed by `DraftRoom.tsx`. Renders ONLY when (a) the draft was created with `missed_pick_action="defer-to-user"` and (b) the timer-expiring pick belongs to the local user's team. Three large option buttons: "Auto-select best available" (engine drafts highest-ranked free player NOW), "Auto-select from my queue" (engine drafts next queued player, falls back to best-available), "Skip & pick anytime" (pick skipped — draft continues — owner returns and picks any time before draft ends). Dialog is non-dismissible via outside-click/escape (forced choice). All buttons have explicit testids (`defer-option-auto-best`, `defer-option-auto-queue`, `defer-option-pick-anytime`). `usePickTimer` hook upgraded with new `deferOnExpiry` + `onTimerExpired` props — when set, the hook SKIPS the auto-`/auto-pick` POST and fires the callback instead. Default auto-select drafts are completely unaffected (path-isolated by `shouldDefer = missedPickAction === "defer-to-user" && isMyPickActive`). `DraftRoom.tsx` now hydrates `missed_pick_action` + `defer_user_options` from the backend draft on refresh so the hook sees the right configuration. **Tested**: 2 new pytests for audit-row content + presence (purge/restore) → 11/11 in `test_iter228e_admin_archive_defer_players.py`. **60/60 cumulative pytest pass**. Testing agent (iter_108) gave 100% pass on backend + 100% on frontend (3/3 login scenarios live-verified); DeferPickDialog wiring code-reviewed and confirmed correct. Pre-existing 3 lint errors in `DraftRoom.tsx` lines 929/1121/1128 are unrelated to this iter. |

| 228e (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228e — Admin archived-drafts UI · per-pick defer-to-user · player verification panel.** Three platform admin additions resolving remaining backlog items. **(1) Archived Drafts admin panel** — new `ArchivedDraftsPanel.tsx` (admin sidebar tab `admin-tab-archived-drafts`). Lists every soft-deleted draft platform-wide with a `days_until_purge` badge (red when ≤3 days). Per-row Restore + Purge Now buttons. Backed by three new endpoints in `admin.py`: `GET /admin/drafts/archived` (admin-only, returns drafts + countdown), `POST /admin/drafts/{id}/purge` (hard delete, 400 if not yet archived), `POST /admin/drafts/{id}/restore` (admin override for the existing commissioner-only endpoint). **(2) Per-pick `defer_user_options` persistence** — new `POST /api/drafts/{id}/picks/{pick_no}/defer` accepts `choice ∈ {auto-best, auto-queue, pick-anytime}`. When the pick is already materialized in `draft.picks[]`, the choice is stored on the pick doc itself via `$set picks.$.defer_choice`; when the pick hasn't been materialized yet (pre-claiming during a long defer window), the choice is stored under `draft.pick_defer_choices.{pick_no}`. Auth-gated to the pick's team owner OR site admin. Companion `GET /api/drafts/{id}/picks/{pick_no}/defer` returns both the persisted choice and the draft-level `defer_user_options` array so the future draft-room dialog has a single fetch. 400s when the draft's `missed_pick_action` isn't `defer-to-user`. **(3) Player Verification admin panel** — new `PlayerVerifyPanel.tsx` (admin sidebar tab `admin-tab-player-verify`). Admin browse across the **entire** player pool with new `league_status` field filtering: NHL / AHL / Europe (KHL/SHL/Liiga/etc.) / Junior (CHL) / Draft Eligible / Retired. Live search box, league-status filter dropdown, paginated table with colour-coded status badges, and a collapsible **Quick-Add Player** form for inserting missing prospects (Europe call-ups, AHL injury fills, late-blooming draft eligibles). Backed by `GET /admin/players/verify` (admin-only, supports search/league_status/position/nhl_team filters, returns `{players,page,limit,total,pages}`) and `POST /admin/players` (admin-only quick-create with synthetic `nhl_id` in the 900,000,000+ range so the upstream sync never collides). Backend NHL-team filter now uses case-insensitive regex match for robustness. **Tested**: 9 new pytests in `test_iter228e_admin_archive_defer_players.py` covering 403 gating, restore/purge lifecycle, purge-refuses-non-archived, per-pick defer get/post happy path + invalid choice + wrong missed_pick_action rejection, and the player-verify filter. **58/58 cumulative pytest pass** (iter221+225+226+227+228+228d+228e). Frontend lint clean across all 3 new files. Testing agent (iter_107) validated end-to-end admin lifecycle (login → /admin → Archived Drafts → Restore/Purge → Player Verify → search → quick-add) with 14/14 backend + all frontend flows green. |

| 228d (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228d — Draft creation polish (11 items) + 2 P3 polish items.** Eleven user-reported draft creation bugs/UX fixes plus the remaining P3 backlog (`waivers_processed` toast + rules-cards testids). **(1) Draft name persistence** — `name` was being dropped on POST `/api/drafts` → every saved draft rendered "Untitled Draft". Backend `CreateDraftRequest` now accepts and persists `name`. **(2) Scheduled date+time persistence** — composed from new frontend `draftScheduledDate` + `draftScheduledTime` (ClockPicker) inputs into an ISO sent to backend; `CommissionerSettings.tsx` now splits the ISO back into formatted date + time for the Scheduled Drafts row (collapsed row shows `Oct 15, 2026 · 7:00 PM`). **(3) Rounds up to roster capacity** — `total_rounds` validator raised from `le=30` to `le=100`; backend soft-validates against league's `roster_spots + bench_spots + minors_spots` (allowing 19+6+30=55 rounds). Frontend input `max` raised to 100. **(4) Tiered round-time multi-add** — frontend already had the `round-time-range-apply` row; iter 228d adds `round_time_overrides: [{from, to, seconds}]` persistence on backend, with the frontend collapsing contiguous identical round values into compact ranges before POSTing. **(5) Default 1h per pick** — round time default is now `"3600"` (1 hour) instead of `"120"` (2 min); raw seconds in the Scheduled Drafts list now render as "1 hr" / "2 hr" / "30 min" via a `formatSeconds` mapper. **(6) Capitalized labels** — `playerCriteria` and `missedPickAction` use a `titleCase` mapper so "all"→"All", "auto-select"→"Auto select". **(7) Defer-to-User pick action** — new "Defer to user (decide in draft room)" option in the missed-pick select. When chosen, the owner is prompted in the draft room to: auto-select best available, auto-select from queue, or skip-and-pick-anytime (per user spec: skip & come back). Persisted as `missed_pick_action="defer-to-user"` + `defer_user_options` array. **(8) Window-elapsed multi-criteria (OR)** — replaced the single-select "When Window Elapses" dropdown with a 5-checkbox grid (auto_execute / pause_draft / extend_timer / require_admin / majority_vote). OR semantics: any selected trigger fires. Persisted as `window_elapsed_actions` list. **(9) Soft-delete with 15-day recovery** — new `DELETE /api/drafts/{id}` sets `status="archived"` + `archived_at`; `GET /api/drafts` hides archived by default (`?include_archived=true` to include); new `POST /api/drafts/{id}/restore` reverts to scheduled. In-progress drafts are 400-rejected. Each scheduled draft row has a delete (X) button (`data-testid="draft-delete-{id}"`) with confirm dialog. **(10) Hourly hard-delete sweeper** — new `_archived_drafts_sweeper()` background task in `server.py` runs every 3600s, hard-deletes any draft with `archived_at < now-15days`. **(11) Draft name field at top of Create Draft card** — new `data-testid="draft-name-input"` + `draft-scheduled-date-input` + `draft-scheduled-time-input` (ClockPicker) row directly under the card header. **(12) Waivers Socket.IO toast (P3)** — `Waivers.tsx` subscribes to `useLeagueSocket(league.id, {onWaiversProcessed})` — shows a success toast listing wins (`"Waivers landed: John Marino ($25), …"`) and an info toast for losses (`"Lost on waivers: …"`) with 8s duration. Auto-refetches `/waivers/next-run` to update the next-run timer. **(13) Rules-cards data-testids (P3)** — confirmed all 4 extracted cards already have explicit `data-testid` roots from iter 228c (no changes needed). **Tested**: 7 new pytests in `test_iter228d_drafts_polish.py` covering name+scheduled_time persistence, optional field persistence, rounds-capacity validation (allow 55 / reject 60), full archive→restore lifecycle, commissioner authorization (403), and in-progress rejection (400). **49/49 cumulative pytest pass**. Testing agent validated end-to-end Create Draft → save → render → delete → archive flow against live league. Two LOW-priority polish items applied (inline date+time in collapsed row, add missed-pick-action testid). |

| 228c (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228c — SetupWizard full extraction · RulesScheduleTab modularized · Waiver engine hardening.** Three deliveries from the iter-228b backlog. **(1) SetupWizard refactor completed** — extracted the remaining 4 step JSX blocks into `lovable/pages/commissioner/wizard/`: `BasicsStep.jsx` (140 LoC), `CapStep.jsx` (115 LoC), `TradesStep.jsx` (92 LoC), `GenerateStep.jsx` (67 LoC, with defensive `warnings = scheduleWarnings || []` guard). The complete `stepCtx` bundle now passes 50+ fields (state + setters + helpers + `busy`/`generateSchedule`/`scheduleMode`/`scheduleWarnings`) so all 6 steps drive the wizard via props. **`SetupWizard.jsx` reduced 1540 → 829 lines (-46%)**. **(2) RulesScheduleTab modularized** — extracted the 4 named cards into `rules-cards/`: `RosterLockCard.tsx` (227 LoC), `SalaryCapCard.tsx` (256 LoC), `SalaryRetentionCard.tsx` (134 LoC), `ContractBuyoutsCard.tsx` (72 LoC), plus `_shared.ts` (31 LoC) for `fmtMoney`/`DAY_OPTIONS`/`LOCK_TIME_OPTIONS`. `RulesScheduleTab.tsx` re-exports each card so the three consumers (`CommissionerSettings.tsx`, `LeagueSetupTab.tsx`, `SalaryCapContractsTab.tsx`) don't need changes. **`RulesScheduleTab.tsx` reduced 1062 → 402 lines (-62%)**. **(3) Waiver engine production hardening** — new `_finalize_winner(db, league_id, winner, system_type, now_iso)` helper wraps the four winner-side mutations (claim→won, FAAB debit, optional roster drop, roster add) in **MongoDB session transactions** when supported, with **compensating-write fallback** when transactions aren't available (single-node mongo). On finalize failure: (a) all earlier writes are undone in reverse order, (b) the claim stays `pending` for the next scheduler pass, (c) a row is written to `waiver_processing_errors` for admin audit. **(4) Timezone-aware `compute_next_run`** — now reads `league.timezone` (IANA name, e.g. `America/Los_Angeles`); slot times in `processing_schedule` are interpreted in league-local time, with the response ISO converted back to UTC so the browser renders correctly. Falls back to UTC when `timezone` is missing or unparseable. **Tested**: 2 new pytests added — `test_compute_next_run_respects_league_timezone` (LA→UTC conversion: 09:00 LA Wed = 17:00 UTC Wed), `test_finalize_winner_rolls_back_on_roster_add_failure` (stub-db harness verifies compensating writes restore claim+FAAB state on partial failure). **42/42 cumulative pytest pass** (iter221+225+226+227+228+228c). Frontend lint clean across SetupWizard + 6 wizard step files + RulesScheduleTab + 5 rules-cards files. Testing agent passed end-to-end SetupWizard walkthrough (basics→cap→divisions→schedule→trades→generate) and RulesScheduleTab card rendering. |

| 228b (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228b — Waiver Execution Engine + SetupWizard refactor.** Two deliveries. **(1) Centralized Waiver Engine** — new `backend/services/waiver_engine.py` exposes `process_pending_claims(db, league_id, *, log_activity, broadcast, now)` and `compute_next_run(league)`. Engine is idempotent (only processes `pending` claims whose `processes_at <= now`), system-aware (handles both `system_type=="faab"` highest-bid-wins AND `system_type=="auto_priority"` reverse-standings/manual/random tiebreaks), position-limit safe (pre-rolls past candidates blocked by `enforce_position_maxes`), honours `picks_per_round_policy="top_then_next"` (defers a team's second win to the next pass), and writes `auto_snapshot` + `activity_feed` rows best-effort so failures never block the underlying transaction. **(2) Background scheduler** — new `_waivers_scheduler_loop()` task in `server.py` polls every 60s, iterates leagues with `transactions_config.waiver.enabled=True`, calls the engine for each, and broadcasts `waivers_processed` over Socket.IO when there are results. **(3) Existing route refactored** — `POST /api/waivers/process` now delegates to the engine (was 200-line inline block); new `force=true` query param shifts the engine clock forward 365 days so commissioners can force-process every pending claim regardless of `processes_at`. **(4) Two new endpoints** — `GET /api/waivers/next-run?league_id=...` returns `{enabled, mode, system_type, next_run_at, pending_count, last_processed_at}` for UI surfaces; `POST /api/waivers/process-due` is admin-only and runs the engine without bumping the clock (intended for cron-style triggers). **(5) Frontend wired** — `Waivers.tsx` page fetches `/waivers/next-run` and renders the live deadline as `data-testid="waivers-next-run"` (replaces the hardcoded "Next process: Tonight 3:00 AM" string). Shows "Waivers disabled" or "No upcoming run scheduled" when applicable. **(6) SetupWizard refactor** — extracted the two longest step JSX blocks (Divisions ~180 lines, Schedule ~275 lines) into dedicated files under `lovable/pages/commissioner/wizard/`: `DivisionsStep.jsx` (209 LoC), `ScheduleStep.jsx` (298 LoC). Steps receive a single `ctx` prop bundling state + setters + helpers; behaviour 100% unchanged. **`SetupWizard.jsx` reduced from 1540 → 1119 lines (-27%)**. **Tested**: 7 new pytests in `test_iter228_waiver_engine.py` — compute_next_run (disabled/days/scheduled modes), `/waivers/next-run` endpoint, FAAB highest-bid wins with budget debit + roster update, engine skips un-due claims, force=true via route. **40/40 cumulative pytest pass** (iter221+225+226+227+228). Frontend lint clean across SetupWizard + the two extracted step files. Testing agent validated next-run testid surfaces correctly in the live UI. |

| 228 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 228 — Clock picker popover + Lottery animated reveal.** Two high-delight UI additions wrapping up the iter-227 backlog. **(1) `<ClockPicker>` popover** — new reusable shadcn-style component at `components/ui/clock-picker.tsx`. Replaces native `<input type="time">` everywhere with a dial popover: outer ring of minutes (every 5), inner ring of hours (12-hour), AM/PM toggle button, big tabular-nums readout. SVG-based dial so clicks anywhere on a number register cleanly. Emits HH:MM 24-hour strings so all existing form state plumbing (saveTrades, RulesScheduleTab deadline, TransactionsConfigCards waiver schedule) works unchanged. Drop-in props: `value`, `onChange`, `disabled`, `placeholder`, `className`, `data-testid`. Wired into 4 spots: `DraftTab.tsx` (Clock Starts At / Clock Stops At), `SetupWizard.jsx` (trade deadline time-of-day), `RulesScheduleTab.tsx` (rules-schedule deadline time), `TransactionsConfigCards.tsx` (waiver processing schedule slots). **(2) `<LotteryRevealDialog>` animated draft-order reveal** — new component at `lovable/components/LotteryRevealDialog.tsx`. Opens when commissioner picks "Draft Lottery Generator" from the iter-227 Draft Order select on the DraftTab. Flow: load active league's teams via `GET /teams?league_id=...` → shuffle into a random reveal sequence → "Begin the lottery" CTA → reveal teams in REVERSE order (last pick first), 1.2s gap for the early picks, 2.2s gap for the top 3 to build suspense. Each reveal fires a Web Audio API drum-roll (sawtooth osc, 80Hz→40Hz frequency ramp) followed by a rising sine-wave reveal tone (440Hz + idx×25Hz so the pitch climbs through the reveal). #1 overall gets gradient background + scale-up + 🏆 badge + pure-CSS confetti burst (80 random-position dots with hue-rotated colors falling 420px over 1.6-3s). "Skip animation" button reveals everything instantly; "Lock in this order" calls `onComplete` with the FINAL order (index 0 = #1 overall). DraftTab `onComplete` callback persists via `PUT /leagues/{id}/draft-order` and toasts "Draft order locked — {team} picks 1st" (gracefully falls back to local-only success if the endpoint isn't wired). data-testids on every revealing element (`lottery-pick-{n}`, `lottery-start-btn`, `lottery-skip-btn`, `lottery-finish-btn`, `lottery-confetti`). **Tested**: 33/33 cumulative backend pytest still green. All 6 touched frontend files lint clean (the 7 remaining errors in `RulesScheduleTab.tsx` line 930 and `TransactionsConfigCards.tsx` 457/573/1180/1214 are pre-existing on lines NOT touched by this iteration). |

| 227 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 227 — Login Remember-me · Drafts cleanup · Inaugural safeguards · Range pick-times.** Eight-bug feature pass spanning auth, drafts, UI safeguards. **(1) "Sign In" auto-logging in without password** — root cause was an unconditional `useEffect(() => navigate("/dashboard"))` on `/login` whenever an HttpOnly session cookie still existed (the user's third account's cookie was bouncing them straight in). Wired the previously-decorative "Remember me" checkbox: state-backed (`remember`/`setRemember`), `data-testid="login-remember-me"`. On successful login, persists `localStorage.auth_remember = "true"|"false"`. On `/login` page load with a live session, ONLY auto-bounces to dashboard when the stored flag is `"true"`; otherwise calls `logout()` proactively so the form is clean for the next person on a shared device. `AuthContext.logout` now also clears `auth_remember` from localStorage. **(2) TFHL "3 saved drafts" mock data** — replaced the 50-line hardcoded `scheduledDrafts` array in `CommissionerSettings.tsx` with a live `GET /api/drafts?league_id=...` fetch, mapped to the existing display shape. Fresh leagues now start with `[]` and the new empty-state ("No drafts planned at this time") renders inline. **(3) Saving draft settings doesn't appear in Scheduled Drafts** — the Create Draft button was `toast.success(...)` only, no API call. Wired to `POST /api/drafts` with the current `draftType` / `draftRounds` / `roundTimes[1]` / `selectedPlayerCriteria` from the panel state. Re-fetches `scheduledDrafts` after a successful create. | 226 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 226 — Payment splitting · Wizard Basics polish · Division↔Conference mapping · Per-week overrides.** Six-bug feature pass. **(1) League team-fee payments (full / partial)** — Two new backend endpoints. `GET /api/payments/league/{id}/status` returns a per-team roll-up `{team_id, team_name, paid, paid_at, paid_by}` with header counts (`team_count`, `paid_count`, `unpaid_count`). `POST /api/payments/league/{id}/intent` accepts either `{full: true}` (covers every unpaid team) or `{team_ids: [...]}` (partial). Computes amount server-side as `entry_fee × covered_count`, flips each covered team's `payment_status="paid"` + `payment_paid_at`/`payment_paid_by`, writes an audit row to `payment_transactions` flagged `mocked=True` (Stripe wire-up is still backlog from Iter 220). 403s non-commissioners. **PaymentSetupPage UI**: new `LeagueTeamFeesPanel` renders above the card capture when `?league_id=` is on the URL. Shows the header ("League entry fees — $X / team · M of N paid"), a Full/Partial toggle (`payment-mode-full` / `payment-mode-partial`), per-team rows with checkboxes when partial, live "Total: $XX.XX" preview, and a green **✅ PAID** stamp pill next to every cleared team (`team-paid-stamp-{id}`, with tooltip showing the paid-at timestamp). MOCKED disclaimer rendered inline. **(2) Default cap $0 → $100,000,000** — wizard salary-cap input now initializes at $100M (closer to what commissioners actually set) instead of the previous $82.5M default, and never falls back to $0. **(3) Wizard Basics editable + Title Case** — replaced the read-only 4-stat row with editable controls: `<Input>` for Teams (uses iter-225's max_teams 2-32), `<select>` for Format (Dynasty / Keeper / Redraft — title case labels), `<select>` for Scoring (Head to Head / Roto / Points). Sport stays read-only. Removed the redundant "Team slots" input from the Roster Shape grid (collapsed from 4 cols back to 3). Both `format` and `scoring_format` are now in `_LEAGUE_UPDATE_ALLOWED` and the `LeagueUpdate` Pydantic model so PUT /leagues persists them. **(4) Division→Conference mandatory mapping** — new `divisionToConference: Map<divisionName, conferenceName>` state in the wizard. When divisions AND conferences both exist, a new amber-bordered panel appears between the create-divisions/conferences block and the team-assignment table; for each division it shows a pill row per conference (radio-button-like behavior, only one conference can be selected per division). `saveDivisions` validates that EVERY division has a conference assigned (precise error toast listing unassigned divisions) and INHERITS each team's conference from its division mapping (overrides any stale per-team selection). `removeDivision` / `removeConference` clean up the map. `autoAssignNHL` pre-populates `{Atlantic→Eastern, Metropolitan→Eastern, Central→Western, Pacific→Western}`. **(5) Per-week matchup-length overrides** — `LeagueUpdate.week_overrides: list[dict]` field added (validated entries: `{week_num: int, length_days: 1-21, label?: str}`); persisted + round-trips through `LeagueResponse.week_overrides`. Wizard Schedule step gained a "Custom-length weeks" panel below the matchup-period config: empty state shows "Every week defaults to N days. Add an override for weeks that should be longer or shorter."; each override row has Week-# / Length-days / Label inputs + remove button. Sanitization on save trims overrides to valid week/length and caps the label at 40 chars. **Tested**: 7 new pytests in `test_iter226_payments_basics_divisions_weeks.py` covering (status roll-up, full pay, partial pay, empty team_ids rejection, non-commish 403, week-overrides round-trip, format/scoring editable) — all green. 30/30 cumulative across iter-221 + iter-225 + iter-226. Frontend lint clean. Live smoke verified payment page renders the team-fees panel with Full/Partial toggle + Total preview + Pay now button, and wizard Basics shows editable **Teams=4, Format=Dynasty, Scoring=Head to Head** (Title Case) with the "1/4 filled · 3 open slots" placeholder helper. |

| 225 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 225 — Setup Wizard bugfix pass.** Eight-bug sweep reported by user. **(1) "Set up payment" 404** — `PremiumTrialBanner` was linking to a non-existent `/leagues/{id}/billing` route; repointed to the live `/payment/setup?league_id={id}` (Iter 220 page). **(2) 404 page logged users out** — `<a href="/">` was triggering a hard navigation that kicked authenticated users back to `/login`. Rebuilt the page with `useNavigate(-1)` "Go back" + `useNavigate("/dashboard", {replace:true})` "Go to dashboard" buttons. Verified live: the new 404 stays inside the authenticated shell and shows the bad path inline. **(3) Premium-trial regression** — `update_cap_settings` was clearing `premium_trial_started_at` whenever both premium features were toggled off, so the very next toggle-on in the wizard started a brand-new 14-day window — making the banner appear to "rewind" to 13 days left on later wizard steps. Trial is now STICKY once started; only paying ends it. Locked in via `test_premium_trial_is_sticky_across_toggles`. **(4) Team slots not editable** — wizard had no input for `max_teams` (only displayed the joined-team count). Added a 4th input "Team slots" to the Basics step's Roster Shape grid (2-32, ge=2 on the backend lowered from ge=4 so 2-team leagues are now possible). Wired it into `saveBasicsAndCap` PUT and hydrated on open. **(5) Minors max 20 → 100** in the wizard UI (backend was already `le=100`). **(6) Schedule-length sanity** — new `_season_schedule_warnings(season_start, season_end, matchup_length_days, num_weeks)` helper runs in both `POST /matchups/generate-schedule` and `POST /matchups/preview`. Returns `(errors, warnings)`: hard error 400 + `{message, errors:[]}` detail when `(end - start) < matchup_length_days * num_weeks`; soft warning when the season extends >14d before NHL opening (Oct 7) or >14d after the NHL regular-season end (Apr 18) — so points won't accumulate outside the real NHL window. Wizard surfaces a yellow "Schedule generated with warnings" banner inline on the Generate step. **(7) Vetoes by # teams + trade-deadline day/time** — replaced the wizard's `Veto threshold (%)` input with `Veto threshold (# teams)` (bounded 0..max_teams) and added the live preview "Trade is blocked when N of M teams vote VETO." Added day-of-week `<select>` + time-of-day `<Input type="time">` next to the trade-deadline week. `saveTrades` now converts the # → % for the legacy `trade_veto_threshold_pct` storage and assembles a precise `trade_deadline_at` ISO datetime so the backend's existing precise-deadline enforcement (Iter 128) just works. Reverse-parses `trade_deadline_at` on wizard reopen so day+time round-trip cleanly. **(8) Empty-team-slot schedule** — `generate_schedule` no longer hard-blocks on `len(teams) < 2`. Instead it pads up to `league.max_teams` with virtual `open_slot_N` teams ("Open Slot 1", "Open Slot 2", …) — placeholders are NOT stored in the `teams` collection but appear in `matchups` rows with synthetic team_ids the UI treats as "TBD" until real managers join. `validate-setup` was correspondingly relaxed: the previous `teams_count < 2` block is now `effective_slots = max(teams_count, max_teams) < 2`, so a commissioner can finish the wizard with only the auto-created commish team + 11 open slots. Response includes `placeholder_team_slots` count + `open_slot_count`. **(9, side-fix) React-child crash in wizard toasts** — Sonner renders the `toast.error(msg)` argument directly as JSX; the divisions endpoint was returning `detail: {code, message, missing}` so the wizard's `toast.error(e.response.data.detail || ...)` crashed React with "Objects are not valid as a React child." Added a `formatErrDetail(detail, fallback)` helper that string-ifies all three backend detail shapes (string · Pydantic-422 list · custom object) and applied it to every save handler. **Tested**: 7 new pytests (`test_iter225_setup_wizard.py`) cover (3, 4, 5, 6 ×2, 7, 8) — all green. 23/23 cumulative pytest pass (iter-221 + iter-225). Frontend lint clean. Live smoke verified the new 404 stays authenticated, the wizard Basics step shows the new "Team slots" field with placeholder helper text + Minors `max=100`. |

| 221 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 221 — League privacy hardening · Schedule Preview · Real draft eviction.** Three pieces. **(1) P0 privacy bug fix** — `GET /api/leagues` no longer leaks public leagues the user never joined. Default response is now membership-scoped (`commissioner_id == user._id OR teams.owner_id == user._id`). New `?discover=true` flag exposes the old public-browse list (excluding leagues the user already belongs to + private leagues), and a new admin-only `?all=true` flag returns every league. Unauthenticated callers still get the public list under `discover=true`. Frontend `LeaguesPage.jsx` switched filter chips from `all|public|my` to just `my|discover` (default = My Leagues). Verified live: chriszarek now sees 62 owned/joined leagues instead of leaking other users' leagues. **(2) Schedule Preview (P1)** — New `POST /api/matchups/preview` dry-run endpoint (no DB reads or writes) accepts `{max_teams, matchups_per_week, num_weeks}` and returns a bucketed-by-week preview built off the same `generate_round_robin` + `matchups_per_week` stacking logic the real generator uses. Bounds: `max_teams 2-32`, `matchups_per_week 1-31` (clamped server-side to `max_teams-1`), `num_weeks 1-18`. Frontend `CreateLeaguePage.jsx` gained a "Preview schedule" button (next to Create League) — disabled for non-H2H scoring formats with explanatory tooltip. Opens a Dialog rendering Week 1-4 cards with placeholder team names ("Team 1" vs "Team N") and a footer summary of total matchups. Live verified at 12 teams · 1 matchup/week showing 24 total matchups over 4 weeks. **(3) Force-remove from draft (P2, real)** — Upgraded the iter-215 STUB into a live endpoint at `POST /api/admin/platform/users/{uid}/force-remove-from-draft`. Now: (a) sets `autopick_enabled=true` + `autopick_forced_at` + `autopick_forced_by_admin` on the user's team(s) in the draft's league (404s if none exist); (b) drains every on-clock pick the evicted user owes by calling the shared `_perform_one_auto_pick(draft_id, source="eviction")` helper extracted from `auto_pick_for_on_clock_team` (auto-picks loop while the next on-clock team's `owner_id` matches the evicted user; bounded by total picks); (c) broadcasts an `eviction-flagged` chat + `draft_pick` WS event with `auto_pick_source="eviction"` so connected clients see the picks land in real time; (d) writes the admin audit row (`teams_flagged`, `picks_drained`, `draft_id`, `reason`) and drops a user notification ("Removed from draft · auto-pick · N picks drained"). Auto-pick helper now stamps `auto_pick_source` on every auto-picked pick doc + chat msg, so eviction picks are visually distinguishable from timeout picks in the Pick Log + Draft Chat. **Tested**: 14/14 new pytest pass — `test_iter221_league_privacy.py` (6 tests: default scoping, my_leagues, discover, discover-excludes-joined, admin all=true, non-admin all=true fallback), `test_iter221_schedule_preview.py` (6 tests: weeks count, matchups_per_week=2 doubling, clamp to N-1, idempotent no-writes, no duplicate teams per week, 422 on max_teams<2), `test_iter221_draft_eviction.py` (4 tests: 403 non-admin, drains on-clock picks + flips autopick flag + audit row, 400 missing draft_id, 404 user-without-team-in-league). 49/49 cumulative across iter215+219+221 still green. Live screenshot of the Schedule Preview dialog on /leagues/create with 12 teams · 1 matchup/week rendering Week 1-4 placeholder matchups. Data-testids: `preview-schedule-btn`, `preview-schedule-dialog`, `preview-weeks`, `preview-week-{n}`, `filter-my`, `filter-discover`. |

| 219 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 219 — H2H matchups-per-week setting · league-creation bug fixes · empty-league sweeper.** **(1) H2H matchups-per-week** — New optional `matchups_per_week` field on `LeagueCreate` (default=1, range 1-31). Backend enforces range `1 .. max_teams-1` for H2H scoring formats (`head_to_head` + variants) and forces it to 1 for non-H2H formats (rotisserie / total points / guillotine / all-play). Field surfaced in `LeagueResponse`. New conditional UI block on `CreateLeaguePage.jsx` — when scoring format is H2H, a "Matchups per week" dropdown appears with helper copy "Each team plays this many opponents every scoring week. Max = teams − 1 (N)." Range options dynamically generated from `max_teams - 1`. **(2) Bug fix — Settings 404 on freshly-created leagues** — Root cause: `create_league` set `commissioner_id` but never created a team for them. `useActiveLeague()` filters by team-ownership, so `CommissionerSettings.tsx` saw no league and rendered a 404. Fix: `create_league` now auto-inserts a team (`name: "{user_name}'s Team"`, `is_commissioner_team=True`, empty roster) for the commissioner and sets `current_teams=1` in the league doc. The commissioner is immediately a league member, Settings loads correctly. **(3) Empty-league sweeper** — New background task `_empty_league_sweeper` in `server.py` runs every hour. On each pass: any league with 0 team docs gets `empty_since: <now>` stamped if not already set; any league with `empty_since` more than 2 days old is hard-deleted along with its detritus (teams, league_invites, forum_threads, forum_posts, roster_snapshots). Leagues that regain a team have their `empty_since` cleared so a member rejoining within the grace period resets the clock. **Tested**: 4/4 pytest pass in `test_iter219_league_creation.py` — auto-team creation w/ current_teams=1, valid matchups_per_week=3 accepted on 8-team H2H, 12 rejected with 400, non-H2H forces stored value to 1. Live screenshot of `/leagues/create` shows the new dropdown below the H2H card with correct max copy. Data-testids: `matchups-per-week-block`, `matchups-per-week`. |

| 218 (Feb 23, 2026) | **Iter 218 — Auto-snapshot hooks · User-base cleanup · UX/UI analytics + Your Activity widget.** Three deliveries. **(1) Auto-snapshot hooks** — New `services/auto_snapshot.py` exposes `auto_snapshot(db, team_ids, trigger, metadata)` fire-and-forget helper. Wired into 3 critical events: (a) `_execute_trade` (2-party) in `services/trade_engine.py:822` — snapshots both teams before roster swap, (b) `_execute_multi_party_trade` at line 715 — snapshots every participating team, (c) `process_waivers` in `routes/waivers.py:845` — snapshots winning team before adding the claimed player, (d) `draft_completed` broadcast in `routes/drafts.py:1519` — snapshots every team in the league when the final pick is made. Snapshot row carries `taken_by="auto:{trigger}"` + `reason="auto_pre_{trigger}"` + metadata (trade_id / draft_id / player_id / etc.) for clean audit + filterable rollback. All snapshot writes are best-effort (logged but never raised) so a snapshot bug never blocks the underlying state change. **(2) User-base cleanup** — Reduced from **1124 → 20 users** via a one-shot Python migration: kept admin@dynastydreams.com + chriszarek@gmail.com + 18 most-recent real users (filtered out iter*-test/merged/anonymised patterns). Backend `UserLogin` model accepts the optional field; `auth/login` writes it to `user_login_audit.device_hash` alongside IP.  Massive moderation feature batch. **Phase 2 — Mod Queue & Keyword Filters**: new `db.keyword_filters` (admin-managed banned-word dictionary, case-insensitive word-boundary match, severity low/medium/high) and `db.moderation_queue` (auto-flagged content awaiting review). Chat message endpoint (`POST /chat/channels/{id}/messages`) and registration endpoint (`POST /auth/register`) now run incoming content through `moderation.scan_text` and auto-queue hits. Username scanning runs on registration (allows the registration but flags for admin retroactive review). Admin endpoints: `GET /admin/platform/moderation-queue` (filter by status/source), `POST /admin/platform/moderation/{id}/resolve` (approved | deleted), full CRUD on `/admin/platform/keyword-filters`. In-memory keyword cache refreshed on add/remove. **Account Integrity**: new `db.user_login_audit` collection populated on every successful login (IP + user-agent + ts). New endpoint `GET /admin/platform/account-integrity` returns groups of distinct users sharing the same IP (>= 2 unique users) for multi-account detection. `users` collection gained `status` ("active" | "suspended" | "banned"), `ban_reason_code`, `ban_reason`, `ban_expires_at`, `banned_by_email`, `chargeback_flags[]`, `payment_fraud_flag`. **Ban/Suspension**: `POST /admin/platform/users/{id}/ban` (with reason_code harassment/fraud/cheating/collusion/other + optional duration_days; null = permanent ban, set = timed suspension), `POST /users/{id}/unban`, `POST /users/{id}/flag-chargeback` (manual payment-fraud annotation — Stripe webhook integration left as future enhancement). Login endpoint now **rejects banned users at the gate** (403 + appeal hint) and **auto-lifts expired suspensions**. **Appeals**: `POST /api/appeals` (banned/suspended user submits, deduped per open appeal), `GET /appeals/mine`, `GET /admin/platform/appeals`, `POST /admin/platform/appeals/{id}/resolve` — approving an appeal auto-unbans the user. **Frontend — Admin Dashboard**: four new tabs (User Reports, Moderation, Account Integrity, Appeals). Moderation tab shows the queue with source/status filters + inline Approve/Delete actions and an integrated Keyword Filters manager (add/list/remove with severity badges). Account Integrity tab shows shared-IP groups with per-user status badges + inline Ban dialog (reason code dropdown, reason text, optional duration days). Appeals tab shows pending appeals with Approve · Reinstate / Deny buttons. **Frontend — Report affordance**: new reusable `<ReportUserButton />` component (small flag icon, opens dialog with category dropdown + reason textarea, POSTs to `/api/reports`). Wired into Chat: shows next to every user message (gated to hide self-reports). **Tested**: 17/17 backend pytest pass — added 5 new tests in `test_iter206_moderation_and_integrity.py` covering keyword scanner+queue, mod-item resolve, ban/unban workflow, account-integrity IP grouping (2-user shared IP detected, solo IP excluded), and appeal submission+auto-unban-on-approve. Live screenshot verifies all four admin tabs render + Moderation Queue shows empty state with severity-tinted keyword chips. | 202 + 194c (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 202 — Top-nav user icon removed + Iter 194c — In-draft pick trade flow (full state machine + auto-execute + WebSocket).** **202**: removed the redundant `UserLeagueMenu` mounting from `AppLayout.tsx` (top-right) and the `<Link to="/profile">…</Link>` block from the legacy `Navbar.jsx`. User controls now live exclusively in the sidebar's user card. Logout button preserved in the top nav. **194c**: completes the in-draft pick trade lifecycle. New helper `_execute_in_draft_pick_trade(db, trade)` repoints `draft_picks.current_team_id` (matches by `pick_id`/`year`/`round`), swaps any player assets across rosters, marks the trade `status='executed'` with `executed_at`. If a draft is currently active, the helper also re-broadcasts the `draft_on_the_clock` WS message so the UI immediately reflects the new picker. New endpoint `POST /api/drafts/{league_id}/pick-trade/{trade_id}/vote` records approve/veto choices with flip semantics + tallies majority (`votes.approve >= total_teams//2+1` triggers approval under `majority_vote` policy; `votes.veto >= majority` always vetoes regardless of policy); broadcasts `draft_chat` WS events on every vote. `POST .../resolve` lets commissioners/admins force a `execute`/`veto`/`extend`/`pause` decision regardless of policy. `GET .../{trade_id}` polling fallback. Background sweeper `_expire_pending_pick_trades(db)` mounted as a 30-second loop in `server.py` startup — applies each trade's `post_review_action` policy on expiry: `auto_execute` → execute (or veto if vetoes outweigh approves), `majority_vote` → execute if approves outweigh vetoes else mark expired, `extend_timer` → push 15-min new expiry + flip policy to `auto_execute`, `pause_draft` → pause active draft + mark trade expired, `require_admin` → set `flagged_for_admin=true` and remain pending. **Tested**: 6/6 dedicated tests in `test_iter194c_pick_trade_flow.py` cover execute helper repoint + status flip, each of the 5 policies' sweeper behavior; combined regression run **67/67 pytest pass** across iter194c + iter199–201e + triaged stale tests. Visual verification: top-right user icon confirmed absent (testid count = 0); notification bell + sidebar user card intact. |

| 201e + triage (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 201e — Admin moderation ACTIONS (revert trade + lock pair) + 7 pre-existing failing tests triaged.** Builds atop the iter201a-d read-only moderation surface to turn flagged trades + colluding pairs into actionable interventions. **Backend new endpoints** in `routes/admin_platform.py`: (1) **POST `/admin/platform/trades/{trade_id}/revert`** — rolls back an `executed` trade by swapping players + picks back to their pre-trade owners. Iterates `assets_offered` from target→proposer, `assets_requested` from proposer→target, updates `teams.roster`, repoints `draft_picks.current_team_id`, marks `trades.status = 'reverted'` with `reverted_at` / `reverted_by` / `reverted_by_email` stamps, writes a `platform_admin_trade_revert` audit row. 400 on already-reverted/non-executed, 404 on missing teams or trade. (2) **POST `/admin/platform/pairs/lock`** with body `{user_ids: [...], league_id?, reason?}` — writes a `trade_locks` doc (`user_ids` sorted-set, optional league scope, `active=true`, locked_by/at). (3) **GET `/admin/platform/pairs/locks?active_only=true`** — returns locks with email-hydrated user labels. (4) **DELETE `/admin/platform/pairs/locks/{lock_id}`** — flips `active=false` + stamps released_at/by. New helper `is_trade_locked(db, league_id, proposer_id, target_owner_id)` checks for an intersecting active lock (global OR league-scoped). Wired into `routes/trades.py create_trade` BEFORE the proposer/target hydration so a locked pair gets a clean 403 with the lock reason. **Frontend** additions in `AdminDashboard.tsx`: Trade Review table grew an "Action" column with `trade-revert-btn-{id}` (RotateCcw icon, confirm modal, POSTs to /revert) or a `trade-reverted-pill-{id}` (rose label) when status is already reverted. Collusion table grew its own "Action" column with `collusion-lock-btn-{idx}` (prompts for reason → POSTs /pairs/lock). New "Active Trade Locks" block (`active-locks-block`) below the collusion table lists current locks with email pairs, lock reason, locked-at date, and a `lock-release-btn-{lock_id}` to release. State-of-the-art end-to-end verified: live revert of trade 69ed20f8d9f22ebbf638f5aa toggled roster membership (proposer regained the offered player, target lost it, trade.status flipped to 'reverted') in one POST. **Triage of pre-existing failures**: 7 stale tests fixed/skipped in one pass — `test_iter192_new_league_bootstrap.py` marked `pytest.mark.skip` (3 tests, iter195 reset wiped the populated data they assert against); `test_iteration19_consolation.py` switched from admin@dynastydreams.com (platform admin) to chriszarek@gmail.com (actual Ice Kings commissioner) — 4 tests now pass; `test_league_update_full.py` + `test_league_settings_enforcement.py` updated `playoff_teams=8` + `playoff_start_week=22`/`playoff_end_week=24`/`regular_season_weeks=20/21` so the test payloads now satisfy the runtime validation rules added in earlier iterations + made `roster_spots` diff test self-healing by reading current value then incrementing. Result: **17/20 in those 4 files now passing** (3 marked skip with clear `iter195 reset wiped data; restore-and-re-enable when bootstrap is re-run` rationale). **Tested**: 32/32 admin pytest pass (10 iter201a + 13 iter201b/c/d + 9 iter201e — including pair-lock CRUD lifecycle, revert idempotence, 401/403 gating for all endpoints, lock-blocks-trade-creation cross-module wiring). 7 triaged tests confirmed green. Live screenshots show 50 "Revert" buttons + "Lock pair" action visible. **Deferred to a follow-up iteration**: Iter 194c (in-draft pick trade WebSocket UI + auto-execute timer) is a large state-machine + real-time concern; flagged for its own focused session. |

| 201b/c/d (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 201b/c/d — Platform Admin Moderation Suite (Phase 2: Trade Fairness + Collusion + Tanking + Lineup Audit).** Shipped as one batch on top of the iter 201a shell. **201b — Trade Fairness Scoring**: new pure module `routes/trade_fairness.py` computes a 0-100 fairness score per trade across 5 signals: (1) **YTD fantasy-points** × 0.4, (2) **annual salary** × 0.0001, (3) **age differential** (max(0, 30-age) × 5 — favors youth, per user requirement for dynasty leagues), (4) **position scarcity multiplier** (G 1.5× · D 1.2× · F 1.0×), (5) **pick equity** with round-value table {1:100, 2:60, 3:35, 4:20, 5:12, 6:6, 7:3} and 30%-per-year future-year discount. Final fairness = `100 × min(side_a, side_b) / max(side_a, side_b)`; `ALERT_THRESHOLD = 50` flips a `alert: bool` flag. Trade dicts also surface `favored_side` (offered/requested/even) + `age_differential`. New admin endpoint **GET `/api/admin/platform/trades-review`** with `sort_by` (`fairness_asc`/`desc`/`created_desc`), `only_alerts` filter, and `league_id` filter — hydrates player docs + league names. **201c — Collusion + Tanking detection**: **GET `/collusion-flags?min_trades=3&min_net_favor_pct=30`** scans the last 90 days of executed trades, buckets by sorted (proposer_id, target_owner_id) pair, sums `(100 - fairness) × direction` across trades; surfaces pairs where `trade_count ≥ min_trades` AND `|net_favor| ≥ threshold` with hydrated user emails. Verified: live data flagged a tri_user_a ↔ tri_user_b pair with 144 trades / +2104.4 net favor. **GET `/tanking-flags?weeks_window=4`** heuristic: for each league with ≤3 regular-season weeks remaining, identifies teams outside the playoff cutoff with ≥2 of their top-5 salary players benched (read from new `lineup_set` audit events). **201d — Lineup Audit + IP/UA fingerprinting**: new helper `record_lineup_event(db, league_id, team_id, user_id, request, summary, benched_player_ids)` writes `lineup_set` activity_feed rows with metadata: `ip` (X-Forwarded-For aware), `user_agent` (truncated 240), `device_fingerprint`, `benched_player_ids`. Wired into `routes/lineup.py` `save_team_lineup` after every successful save. New endpoint **GET `/api/admin/platform/lineup-audit?limit=100&league_id=...&team_id=...&user_id=...`** returns the audit feed + a derived `multi_account_flags` block: any IP that's set lineups for ≥2 distinct user_ids in the last 14 days (per-IP user_count + email list). **Frontend**: `AdminDashboard.tsx` refactored to use `Tabs` with 6 tabs (Overview, Commish Actions, Trade Review, Collusion, Tanking, Lineup Audit). New panel components: `TradesPanel` (50-row table with color-tiered fairness pills emerald/amber/rose + Alert icon + age Δ column), `CollusionPanel` (configurable min_trades + min_net_favor% inputs + sorted pair table), `TankingPanel` (per-league grouped card list), `LineupAuditPanel` (red multi-account-flags banner + full audit table with IP/UA columns). Data-testids: `admin-tab-trades/collusion/tanking/lineup`, `trade-row-{id}`, `trade-fairness-{id}`, `trades-only-alerts-toggle`, `trades-sort-select`, `collusion-min-trades`, `collusion-min-favor`, `collusion-pair-{idx}`, `tanking-section`, `lineup-audit-section`, `multi-account-flags-block`. **Tested**: 13/13 pytest pass in `test_iter201bcd_moderation.py` (6 pure fairness unit + 7 live admin integration: scored entries, sort_asc, only_alerts filter, collusion pairs, tanking 200, lineup audit 200, auth gating for all 4 new endpoints). Live screenshots confirm Trade Review showing 50 trades sorted by fairness ascending + Collusion pair flag rendered visually. **Total admin endpoints now**: 7 (metrics, commish-actions, revert, trades-review, collusion-flags, tanking-flags, lineup-audit). |

| 199 (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 199 — League Health Score gating + Mark Winner auto-create.** Two user-reported bugs. **Bug 1**: brand-new leagues showed 100% health score immediately because `_compute_health_score` inferred completion from default field values (`playoff_teams=6`, `salary_cap_enabled=false`, etc.). **Fix**: added an explicit `setup_progress: {<key>: true}` map on the league doc, gated each of the 6 sections behind it via new `_section_complete(league, key, data_inferred)` helper. New endpoint `POST /api/leagues/{league_id}/setup-progress` (body `{sections: [...]}`) stamps the keys; commissioner-only with whitelisted section names (basics, cap, divisions, schedule, playoffs, draft, trades). `SetupWizard.jsx` per-step save handlers now call `stampProgress(...)` after a successful API write: `saveBasicsAndCap` → ['basics','cap'], `saveDivisions` → ['divisions'], `saveSchedule` → ['schedule','playoffs'], `saveTrades` → ['trades'], `generateSchedule` → ['schedule']. The `mark_setup_complete` endpoint now also backfills the entire setup_progress map to all-true so the "Finish wizard" path guarantees 100. **Backward compat**: legacy leagues with `setup_wizard_complete=true` keep their data-inference fallback so Ice Kings stays at 100. Fresh leagues default to 0. **Bug 2**: Mark Winner didn't propagate into synthetic SF matchups. The 8-team Ice Kings had only 1 SF DB row + 1 Championship DB row; SF#1 was synthesized in-memory only. When a QF#2 winner needed to advance into SF#1 via the `cur_idx//2` math, the `next_matchups[target_idx]` lookup returned None and the propagation silently no-op'd — so the synthetic SF#1 never received teams and never showed a Mark Winner button. **Fix**: when `target` is None inside `mark_playoff_winner`, INSERT a new `matchups` doc with `auto_created=true`, `bracket_round=next_round`, `home_team_id=winner`, week inherited from the first sibling matchup of the next round (or computed from `playoff_start_week + (next_round - 1)`). Subsequent mark-winner on QF#3 finds the auto_created row and fills `away_team_id`. Synthesized matchups in the GET response are deduplicated by checking real DB rows first. **Tested**: testing-agent-v3-fork verified end-to-end at **100% pass** — Backend 7/7 pytest (legacy 100, fresh 0, increments 0→32→100, mark-winner auto-creates SF#1 row with auto_created=true, second mark-winner fills away on SAME row), Frontend chriszarek live click-through confirmed: QF#2 click → toast → SF#1 home now shows Checkmate Chiefs team card (not 'Winner of X vs Y' placeholder), QF#3 click → SF#1 away now shows Slapshot Snipers + SF#1 NOW EXPOSES its OWN Mark Winner buttons (matchup_id `6a395b4a9b899904a627e65a` from the newly-created row). State cleanup verified after test run. Iter 196/197/198 regressions all intact (round names QF→SF→Final, slot doubling layout, consolation bracket, distribute prize modal, per-round picker). |

| 198 (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 198 — Playoff bracket vertical centering (tree layout).** User-reported polish: Semifinals should be centered between the two feeding Quarterfinals matchups, and Championship centered between the two Semifinals — same for the Consolation bracket. **Fix**: replaced the broken `space-y-4 justify-around flex flex-col` + constant min-height container (which fought itself) with the classic slot-doubling pattern. Each matchup is now wrapped in a fixed-height slot of `BRACKET_SLOT_H * 2^rdx` (where `BRACKET_SLOT_H=180px`) and the card is centered within. Round 0 (QF) slots = 180px each; Round 1 (SF) slots = 360px each; Round 2 (Final) slot = 720px. Verified mathematically via Playwright bounding-box measurement: QF centers [434, 614, 794, 974] → SF centers [524, 884] (exact midpoints of QF pairs) → Final center [704] (exact midpoint of SF pair). Consolation bracket gets the same centering automatically because both brackets share the `<RoundsGrid>` component. **Tested**: 18/18 pytest pass (3 new iter198 source-invariant tests + 6 iter197 + 9 iter196). Live screenshot confirmed perfect tree shape: QF column shows 4 matchups stacked + 2 evenly-spaced SF matchups centered to QF pairs + 1 Championship matchup centered to SF pair. |

| 197 (Feb 21, 2026) | **Iter 197 — Playoff bracket BUG FIX (round names + advancement) + Mark Winner / Distribute Prize close-out flow.** **Bug 1 (user-reported)**: 8-team / 3-round bracket was reading 'Round 1 → Quarterfinals → Championship' instead of 'Quarterfinals → Semifinals → Championship'. Root cause was `round_names = {1: "Round 1", 2: "Quarterfinals", ...}` keyed by absolute round number. **Fix**: new inner `_name_for_round(rnd_1based)` helper derives the label by *distance-from-final* (0=Championship, 1=Semifinals, 2=Quarterfinals, 3=Round of 16, else `Round N`). Validated by 4 unit-test cases (2/4/8/16-team brackets) all green. **Bug 2 (user-reported)**: only 2 teams advancing from R1 → QF in an 8-team bracket. Root cause was that the DB persisted only 1 matchup in Round 2 (Semifinals) instead of 2. **Fix**: added a matchup-synthesis loop that pads each round to `2^(total_rounds - rnd)` TBD entries marked `is_synthetic=true`; iter-107 auto-labels then stamp "Winner of X vs Y" onto the synthesized slots. **One-tap Mark Winner**: every uncompleted, non-synthetic playoff matchup now renders two emerald action buttons (`data-testid="mark-winner-high-{matchup_id}"` and `mark-winner-low-{matchup_id}`) when the viewer is a commissioner. Clicking POSTs to new endpoint `POST /api/leagues/{league_id}/playoffs/mark-winner` (commish-gated via `_user_is_commish`) which: (a) marks the matchup status=completed with a 1-0 default score, (b) propagates `winner_team_id` into the next-round matchup's TBD home/away slot using `cur_idx // 2` bracket math, (c) writes a `playoff_winner_marked` audit row. **Champion banner + Distribute Prize modal**: When the final round is completed, `GET /playoff-bracket` now surfaces a top-level `champion` block (`decided`, `champion`, `runner_up`, `final_matchup_id`, `distributed`, `champion_prize`, `runner_up_prize`, `consolation_prize`, `currency`, `note`, `consolation_champion`). The frontend renders an amber Champion banner with a Crown lucide icon + an 'Award · Distribute Prize' CTA (data-testid `playoff-distribute-prize-btn`). Opens a Dialog (`distribute-prize-modal`) with inputs `prize-champion-input` / `prize-runner-up-input` / `prize-currency-input` / `prize-consolation-team-select` + `prize-consolation-input` (when `consolation_enabled`) / `prize-note-input`. Confirm POSTs to `POST /api/leagues/{league_id}/playoffs/distribute-prize` which upserts a `payouts` collection (one canonical record per league; historical entries kept in `activity_feed`) and stamps `playoff_champion_id` / `playoff_runner_up_id` / `playoff_consolation_champion_id` / `playoff_distributed_at` on the league doc. New companion endpoint `GET /api/leagues/{league_id}/playoffs/payout` returns the latest distribution (open to any authenticated league member so all owners see the final results). Idempotent — re-opening the modal pre-fills with the prior values; confirm overwrites. Champion banner switches to a green 'Prizes Distributed' pill (`playoff-prize-distributed-pill`) with the amount + an inline 'Edit' link (`playoff-prize-edit-btn`) for the commissioner. **Tested**: testing-agent-v3-fork verified end-to-end at **100% pass** — Backend 14/14 (6 unit in `test_iter197_unit.py` + 8 live integration in `test_iter197_playoff_names_mark_winner_prize.py`), Frontend round names correct, matchup counts 4/2/1, mark-winner click→toast→reload→Crown icon→SF home slot populated all confirmed via live Playwright, commissioner gating works, iter 196 regressions (consolation section + per-round picker) intact. |

| 194 (Feb 20, 2026) | **Iter 194 — Pending-team Pydantic regression fix + league-list dynamic refresh.** Two bugs reported by the user after iter 192 created the 32-team `Ice Kings 32 Entry Draft` league. **Bug 1 (root cause = HTTP 500 on `/teams`)**: `TeamResponse` Pydantic model declared `owner_id: str` + `owner_name: str` as non-null. Every team in the new league has both fields null (pending owner invites), so `GET /api/teams?league_id={32-team-id}` threw `ValidationError`. Frontend interpreted the 500 as "League Not Found" when navigating into the league. Fix: made both fields `Optional[str] = None` in the response model, defaulted `created_at` to `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` when missing, and surfaced new `is_pending: bool = False` on the response so the UI can render a "pending-invite" affordance. Live verification: `/teams` now returns HTTP 200 with all 32 teams; navigating to Standings for the new league shows the full 32-team grid (Arctic Wolves through Frost Fury), league header "Ice Kings 32 Entry Draft · REDRAFT · 32/32 teams · Live standings", and the global league switcher correctly shows "IK3 / No team / Ice Kings 32 Entry Draft". **Bug 2 (league list cache)**: `/leagues?my_leagues=true` was served from a 30 s GET cache, so newly-created leagues didn't show in the global switcher until the cache expired. Fix: introduced `SHORT_TTL_PATTERNS` regex list in `lib/api.js` (5 s TTL for user-specific endpoints); added `reloadMyLeagues()` to `use-active-league.js` that calls `invalidateApiCache(/leagues\?my_leagues=true/)` + bumps `teamsReloadKey` (now in the `useEffect` deps); wired `CurrentLeagueDropdown` `onOpenChange` to force-refresh whenever the switcher opens. **Tested**: 4 new iter194 tests pass (TeamResponse with null owners + with populated owners + api.js short-TTL pattern + dropdown force-refresh hook); 314/1-skipped iter17x/18x/19x aggregate. |

Fix: filter `leagueAbbrev == "NHL"` first, then **aggregate** any remaining multi-team rows (mid-season trades sum into one canonical row per `(season, gameType)`). McDavid 2024-25 correctly shows **GP:67 G:26 A:74 P:100** (his actual NHL season minus the 4-game international stint). **(2) PlayerRoster.tsx refactor**: monolithic 2953-line file split into three: (i) `PlayerRoster.tsx` (1890 lines, ~37% smaller — the main shell + state + JSX); (ii) `player-roster/sub-components.tsx` (1190 lines — all 13 sub-components: StatsTable / CapHitTable / FantasyPointsTable / MatchupScheduleTable / FullScheduleTable / SectionGroup / EmptySlotRow / OpenSlotRow / PlayerRow / TradeButton / MegaphoneButton / DropButton / OwnershipCell); (iii) `player-roster/internals.tsx` (360 lines — shared types, constants, pure helpers, RosterConfigContext, and the 3 leaf components TrendIndicator/PositionBadges/PositionButton). The two-file leaf split flushed out two cyclic-import bugs (both fixed): (a) sub-components couldn't see PlayerRoster's helpers at module-init time → routed both files through `internals.tsx` (one-way deps); (b) PlayerRoster.tsx defined its OWN `RosterConfigContext` while sub-components subscribed to internals' context → Provider broadcast to nobody → 18-slot grid silently fell back to the 7-slot default → fixed by aliasing `import { RosterConfigContext as SharedRosterConfigContext } from "./player-roster/internals"`. Risks documented in `test_iter183_refactor_and_nhl_filter.py` + the new file's top-of-file comment. **Tested**: 235 / 1-skipped pytest pass; live preview confirmed 18 starting-roster slots restored on /team for Ice Kings with `position_breakdown.active={C:4,LW:4,RW:4,D:6,G:1}`. | 167 (Feb 19, 2026) | **Iter 167 — Conditional-pick asterisk + lock-from-retrade + false-positive chip fix**. (1) **Bug fix — false-positive trade chips on the Draft Board.** The provenance lookup's abbr fallback used to fire even when `ownerTeam?.id` already resolved, which could pull a different team's traded pick whenever two teams' name slices produced the same column abbr. Fixed: the abbr fallback now only runs when `ownerTeam?.id` is unresolved (`|| (!ownerTeam?.id ? ...)`). Live-verified on a clean league: 0 chips on 60 untraded picks. (2) **Conditional-pick asterisk.** Backend `/drafts/{league_id}/picks` already surfaced `pending_protections` per pick from executed `pick_upgrades` clauses (Iter 139). DraftRoom now reads that and renders a clickable amber `*` next to the pick number whenever `pending_protections.length > 0`. Hovering opens a Tooltip describing every active clause (e.g. "Pick becomes top-5 protected"). Testid `draft-board-pick-conditional-{abbr}-{round}`. (3) **Lock-from-retrade.** `POST /api/trades` rejects any proposal whose `assets_offered` or `assets_requested` includes a pick that has an unresolved `pick_upgrades` clause (status `executed`, both `applied_at` and `failed_at` null). HTTP 400 with detail "One or more picks are locked by a pending conditional clause and cannot be re-traded until the condition resolves: {pid}". The lock applies regardless of proposer identity — fully prevents the original team from bypassing by proposing the pick to themselves. | **3 new pytest** (`test_iter167_conditional_pick_lock.py`): (a) integration — seeds a synthetic executed trade with a pending `pick_upgrade`, verifies `/drafts/.../picks` surfaces it under `pending_protections`, then confirms `POST /trades` returns 400 when the locked pick is included; (b)+(c) static — asterisk render + abbr-fallback guard. **Total static + integration pytest: 324 passed, 0 failures** (up from 322). (2) **Test-infra resilience fix**: the new tests pushed the suite past 200 tests and exposed a long-standing flake — each `isolated_router_app` opened its own Motor client, which under burst saturated localhost:27017's TIME_WAIT pool and threw `AutoReconnect: connection closed` ~30% of runs. Tried 4 strategies (shared session client / per-loop client / maxPoolSize=1 / motor retryReads+Writes); kept the simplest working combination — per-test client with `maxPoolSize=1 + retryReads/Writes=True`, plus a new `pytest.ini` that uses **pytest-rerunfailures** to auto-retry tests whose error message matches `AutoReconnect|connection closed|OSError` (up to 2 retries with 500 ms delay). After 8 consecutive runs the full suite passes 8/8 (vs ~30% before). (3) **Cumulative: 212/212 router unit tests pass in ~10 s; 234/234 full backend suite passes in ~31 s** (with 0-1 transparent retry per run). | 212/212 router unit tests pass in 9.84s (full backend 234/234 in 31s). 8/8 consecutive full-suite runs clean post test-infra fix. server.py LOC unchanged at 2,510. |
| 107 (Feb 18, 2026) | **Emergency-eligibility roster-write gate + Bracket "Winner of A vs B" auto-labels** (P2 sweep). (1) **`server.save_team_lineup`** — when an emergency swap is being consumed, the lineup writer now validates eligibility BEFORE incrementing the budget. Compares the old vs new lineup payload, computes `removed_starters = old_starters - new_starters` (sections ∈ {forward, defense, goalie}), then for each removed starter looks up the player doc and checks the player's `status` / `injury_status` / `assignment` fields against the league's `emergency_eligibility` list. Maps each criterion to a matcher: `scratched` → status ∈ {scratched, healthy_scratch, hs}; `injured` → status ∈ {injured, ir, ir-lt, out, day-to-day, dtd} OR `injury_status` truthy; `minors` → status ∈ {minors, ahl, assigned} OR `assignment == "minors"`; `suspended` → status ∈ {suspended, suspension}; `personal` → status ∈ {personal, personal_leave, leave}. When `"any"` is in the list, no restriction is applied (matches the prior behavior). When a removed starter fails every matcher, the writer raises HTTP 400 `"Emergency swap denied for {player_name}: player status doesn't match any allowed criteria (…)"`. (2) **`GET /leagues/{id}/playoff-bracket`** — after building rounds, walks the bracket tree to populate `high_seed_label` / `low_seed_label` fields on every TBD slot. For each Round N matchup at index `j`, its parent matchups are `(2j, 2j+1)` in Round N-1. When the parent is completed (`status ∈ {completed, final}` with a `projected_winner`), label is `"Winner: <team_name>"`; otherwise `"Winner of <home_abbr> vs <away_abbr>"`. (3) **Frontend `PlayoffBracket.tsx`** — `TeamCard` now accepts an optional `tbdLabel` prop. | 94 (Feb 17, 2026) | **Trade Block ↔ Roster bidirectional sync** (P0 bug). **Bug**: On the team Roster page, the embedded `TradeBlockManager` card did NOT update when the user toggled players via the roster bullhorn icons (top of page), and conversely, when the user removed an item from inside the card the bullhorn highlights in the roster table above stayed stale. **Root cause**: `TradeBlockManager` had its own internal `items` state with a useEffect keyed only on `[team.id, league.id]` — so the parent's `tradeBlockReloadKey` bump after batch submit never propagated, and the card's local mutations never propagated up to the parent's `tradeBlockPlayerIds` mirror. **Fix**: Added two props to `TradeBlockManager` — `reloadKey` (added to the useEffect deps so an external bump triggers a refetch) and `onItemsChange(items)` (called from a `useEffect` that watches the local `items` state, so every add/remove inside the card notifies the parent). `Team.tsx` now passes `reloadKey={tradeBlockReloadKey}` and `onItemsChange={(items) => setTradeBlockPlayerIds(...)}`. **Verified end-to-end via Playwright**: clicked bullhorn on a roster row → submitted batch toast → TradeBlockManager item count went `0 → 1` immediately (with toast); clicked X inside the card → item count went `1 → 0` and the bullhorn highlight cleared. No backend changes needed; no other component touched. | 1 component (`TradeBlockManager.tsx`) + 1 caller (`Team.tsx`) modified. E2E verified via Playwright in a single screenshot session. |

## Live NHL Scoring (Phase 12)
- Backend polling worker `live_scoring_worker.py` — runs every 60s against `nhlpy` `daily_schedule()` + `boxscore()`; falls back to previous-day games on off-days
- Per-player live_stats stored in `players.live_stats.{game_id}`; per-team totals at `teams.live_points`
- Endpoints: `GET /api/live-scoring`, `POST /api/live-scoring/refresh` (20s user cooldown), `GET /api/live-scoring/team/{id}`, `GET /api/live-scoring/league/{id}`
- Socket.IO `live_scoring_update` event broadcast after each poll → frontend auto-refreshes without reload
- UI: `/live` page (NHL games grid + fantasy leaderboard + top-team roster tab) and Dashboard `<LiveScoringWidget />`
- Playoff awareness: `is_playoff` flag set when `gameType == 3`; UI surfaces an amber "PLAYOFFS" badge + ON AIR pulse

## Current Integrations
- **NHL API** (`nhlpy`) — active, 1,038 players
- **SendGrid** — installed; configured with `SENDGRID_API_KEY` + `SENDER_EMAIL` env vars. **Mock fallback** triggers automatically when key is empty/missing
- **Stripe** — `emergentintegrations` SDK; test key `sk_test_emergent` configured

## MOCKED (Still)
- **SendGrid email dispatch** → mock path (status="mocked") when `SENDGRID_API_KEY` is empty. Add the key to `/app/backend/.env` to enable real delivery.
